# Article preparation and SEO audit — updated 15 October 2026

3245 saved review manuscripts are present in website editorial source. Their source-pass notes, approval gaps and metadata are retained. All remain unpublished; no new reader-facing article was published. Word counts below include manuscript headings and Markdown link text. This file retains its original 2 October path as a continuously generated audit artifact.

Private launch remains on HOLD and has a noindex directive. Canonical origin and existing route canonicals are preserved. Archival pieces use Article structured data. RSS and news-sitemap records are limited to the existing publication registry. This audit does not prove indexing, ranking, readership, Search Console setup or human fact checking.
